MODEL     : xiaomi/mimo-v2.6-flash
METRIC    : area_delay_product
RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 5.564e+06 area_delay_product  (eval 14)

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

  -> read_file(filename='tb.sv', offset=148)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 3/60]
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 3/60]
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+5], coeffs[b*64+r*8+7]);
        end
      end
      // inter-block gap: >= 2 idle cycles (serializer IDLE+SETUP), varied a bit
      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end
    drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    repeat (400) @(negedge clk);  // flush x + transpose + y pipeline

    if (out_cnt != num_blocks * 64) begin
      $display("TB_ERROR missing outputs: got %0d, expected %0d", out_cnt, num_blocks * 64);
      total_errors = total_errors + 1;
    end
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];



function [5:0] ptr_conv;
    input [5:0] idx;
    reg [5:0] out_idx;
begin
    case (idx)
    6'd0:  out_idx = 6'd0;
    6'd1:  out_idx = 6'd8;

Best so far: 9496575.05048669 area_delay_product (eval 1)

[ASSISTANT]
Baseline: **ADP = 9,496,575** (area 4273.7, delay 2222.1 ps). The critical path is the transpose 32:1 RAM read mux, and area is dominated by ten 32×32 multipliers plus 4096 bits of 32-bit transpose storage.

My optimization plan:
1. **Narrow multiplier operands** (proved safe by input/output range bounds: x-pass outputs ≤ 346,350 → 21 bits suffice; y inputs are exactly these) — 17×13 and 22×13 multipliers instead of 32×32, with identical low-32-bit wrap semantics.
2. **Narrow transpose storage** to 21 bits and y output buffer to 17 bits (proven bounds), with sign extension at the module boundary.
3. Keep the pipeline structure, case schedules, FSM and arithmetic text *identical* to guarantee bit-exact behavior.

Let me write the optimized design:
  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d - full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder\n// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg, Apache 2.0): row pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) ->\n// double-buffered transpose -> column pass (OUT_SHIFT=15), composed\n// exactly as in the original jpeg_idct.v (downstream ready tied high,\n// row pass accepts no backpressure).\n//\n// Pipeline structure, case schedules, FSMs and arithmetic are kept\n// bit-exact with the original RTL. [ASSISTANT]
No reports left behind. Based on the evidence (area dropped only ~95 despite removing ~2000 flops), the area is **combinational-dominated — the ten 32×32 multipliers** — and my operand narrowing didn't propagate because Yosys sign-extends operands to the 32-bit expression context, creating redundant partial-product rows that can't be folded.

The fix: since the B operand is always a *non-negative* 12-bit constant (all C1..C7 < 4096), I can compute:
`a_signed × b = (a_uns × b) − (a_msb ? (b << WA) : 0)` (mod 2^32)
with **zero**-extension (constant zeros → folded by wreduce/abc), giving a true narrow unsigned 17×12 / 22×12 multiplier plus one tiny gated shift and a subtract. This is bit-exact mod 2^32.
  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', old_string="// cosine constants x4096 (all < 4096 => fit signed 13 bit)\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1 = 13'sd4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2 = 13'sd3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3 = 13'sd3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4 = 13'sd2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5 = 13'sd2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6 = 13'sd1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7 = 13'sd799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096", new_string="// cosine constants x4096 (all < 4096 => fit unsigned 12 bit, which is\n// what makes the unsigned-multiply re formulation below exact)\nlocalparam [11:0] C1 = 12'd4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam [11:0] C2 = 12'd3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam [11:0] C3 = 12'd3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam [11:0] C4 = 12'd2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam [11:0] C5 = 12'd2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam [11:0] C6 = 12'd1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096\nlocalparam [11:0] C7 = 12'd799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096")
  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', old_string="reg signed [MA_W-1:0] i0;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul0_a;\nreg signed [12:0]     mul0_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul1_a;\nreg signed [12:0]     mul1_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul2_a;\nreg signed [12:0]     mul2_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul3_a;\nreg signed [12:0]     mul3_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0]     mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk )\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0     <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul0_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul0_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul1_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul1_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul2_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul2_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul3_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul3_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul4_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul4_b <= 13'b0;\nend", new_string="reg signed [MA_W-1:0] i0;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul0_a;\nreg        [11:0]     mul0_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul1_a;\nreg        [11:0]     mul1_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul2_a;\nreg        [11:0]     mul2_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul3_a;\nreg        [11:0]     mul3_b;\nreg signed [MA_W-1:0] mul4_a;\nreg        [11:0]     mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk )\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0     <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul0_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul0_b <= 12'b0;\n    mul1_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul1_b <= 12'b0;\n    mul2_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul2_b <= 12'b0;\n    mul3_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul3_b <= 12'b0;\n    mul4_a <= {MA_W{1'b0}};\n    mul4_b <= 12'b0;\nend")

[ASSISTANT]
Excellent!! **ADP 9,496,575 → 5,808,396 (−39%)**, delay 2222 → 1412 ps, still 100% correct. The read-mux fix worked — new critical path is the `(x * 181) / 256` odd-part scaling in the IDCT passes.

I'll now restructure that computation algebraically: with `x = x_hi*256 + x_lo`, the wrapped product's bits [31:8] equal `low24(181*x_hi + (181*x_lo >> 8))`, and signed truncation needs only a 1-bit adjustment — replacing a deep 32-bit constant-multiply + divide with a shallow 24-bit balanced tree:
  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', new_string="reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\n\n// 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling:  (x * 181) / 256\n//   * the product wraps at 32 bits (two's complement),\n//   * the division is signed and truncates toward zero.\n// With x = x_hi*256 + x_lo (x_hi = x >>> 8, x_lo = x[7:0]):\n//   P = x*181 exactly, W = P (mod 2^32)\n//   U := P[31:8] = low24(181*x_hi + floor(181*x_lo/256))\n//   W[7:0] = P[7:0] = (181*x_lo)[7:0],  W[31] = U[23]\n//   floor(W/256) = sext24(U)\n//   trunc(W/256) = sext24(U) + (W<0 && W[7:0]!=0)\n// so the whole operation is a shallow 24-bit shift-add tree plus a\n// one-bit correction instead of a 32-bit constant multiply + divide.\nfunction signed [31:0] scale_half;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg [7:0]  xl;\n    reg [23:0] xh;\n    reg [15:0] p0;\n    reg [15:0] p1;\n    reg [15:0] p2;\n    reg [15:0] plo;\n    reg [23:0] t1;\n    reg [23:0] t2;\n    reg [23:0] t3;\n    reg [23:0] xh7;\n    reg [23:0] u0;\n    reg [23:0] u1;\n    reg [23:0] vv;\n    reg        adj;\n    begin\n        xl  = x[7:0];\n        xh  = x[31:8];\n        // 181 * xl (exact, <= 16 bit): 128+32+16+4+1\n        p0  = {1'b0, xl, 7'b0} + {3'b0, xl, 5'b0};\n        p1  = {4'b0, xl, 4'b0} + {6'b0, xl, 2'b0};\n        p2  = p0 + p1;\n        plo = p2 + {8'b0, xl};\n        // 181 * xh, low 24 bits (all arithmetic modulo 2^24)\n        t1  = xh + {xh[21:0], 2'b0};\n        t2  = {xh[19:0], 4'b0} + {xh[18:0], 5'b0};\n        t3  = t1 + t2;\n        xh7 = {xh[16:0], 7'b0};\n        u0  = t3 + xh7;\n        u1  = u0 + {16'b0, plo[15:8]};\n        adj = u1[23] & (|plo[7:0]);\n        vv  = u1 + {23'b0, adj};\n        scale_half = {{8{vv[23]}}, vv};\n    end\nendfunction", old_string='reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;')

**Correctness PASS** — my algebraic rewrite is bit-exact (and confirms signed division semantics ✓), but delay got *worse* (1600 vs 1412): I wrote the additions **serially** (6 chained 24-bit CPAs). Area improved slightly (4064). Let me restructure it as a balanced carry-save (CSA) tree — 3 operand stages in parallel, final CPA, then the increment:
  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', new_string="// 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling:  (x * 181) / 256\n//   * the product wraps at 32 bits (two's complement),\n//   * the division is signed and truncates toward zero.\n// With x = x_hi*256 + x_lo (x_hi = x >>> 8, x_lo = x[7:0]):\n//   U := (x*181)[31:8] = low24(181*x_hi + floor(181*x_lo/256))\n//   W[7:0] = (181*x_lo)[7:0],  W[31] = U[23]\n//   floor(W/256) = sext24(U),  trunc(W/256) = sext24(U) + (W<0 && W[7:0]!=0)\n// Implemented as carry-save trees so no serial ripple chain forms.\nfunction signed [31:0] scale_half;\n    input signed   -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', new_string="// Products.\n// The factor operand b is always one of the non-negative constants\n// C1..C7 (12 bit unsigned, value < 4096), so a signed multiply of the\n// sign-extended a with the zero-extended b is bit-identical to the\n// original 32x32 signed wrap-around product.\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul1_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul2_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul4_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk )\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul1_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul2_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul4_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= $signed({{(32-MA_W){mul0_a[MA_W-1]}}, mul0_a}) * $signed({20'b0, mul0_b});\n    mul1_q <= $signed({{(32-MA_W){mul1_a[MA_W-1]}}, mul1_a}) * $signed({20'b0, mul1_b});\n    mul2_q <= $signed({{(32-MA_W){mul2_a[MA_W-1]}}, mul2_a}) * $signed({20'b0, mul2_b});\n    mul3_q <= $signed({{(32-MA_W){mul3_a[MA_W-1]}}, mul3_a}) * $signed({20'b0, mul3_b});\n    mul4_q <= $signed({{(32-MA_W){mul4_a[MA_W-1]}}, mul4_a}) * $signed({20'b0, mul4_b});\nend", old_string="// Products.\n// The factor operand b is always one of the non-negative constants\n// C1..C7 (12 bit unsigned).  Hence\n//    (signed a) * b  ==  a_uns * b - (a_msb ? (b << MA_W) : 0)   (mod 2^32)\n// The unsigned multiply zero-extends its operands (constant zeros in\n// the upper bits), so synthesis builds a genuinely narrow MA_W x 12\n// unsigned multiplier; the result bits re interpreted as two's\n// complement equal the original 32x32 signed wrap-around product.\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul1_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul2_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul4_q;\n\nwire [31:0] ua0 = {{(32-MA_W){1'b0}}, mul0_a};\nwire [31:0] ua1 = {{(32-MA_W){1'b0}}, mul1_a};\nwire [31:0] ua2 = {{(32-MA_W){1'b0}}, mul2_a};\nwire [31:0] ua3 = {{(32-MA_W){1'b0}}, mul3_a};\nwire [31:0] ua4 = {{(32-MA_W){1'b0}}, mul4_a};\n\nwire [31:0] sc0 = mul0_a[MA_W-1] ? ({20'b0, mul2_b} << MA_W) : 32'b0;\nwire [31:0] sc3 = mul3_a[MA_W-1] ? ({20'b0, mul3_b} << MA_W) : 32'b0;\nwire [31:0] sc4 = mul4_a[MA_W-1] ? ({20'b0, mul4_b} << MA_W) : 32'b0;\n\nwire [31:0] pr0 = ua0 * {20'b0, mul0_b} - sc0;\nwire [31:0] pr1 = ua1 * {20'b0, mul1_b} - sc1;\nwire [31:0] pr2 = ua2 * {20'b0, mul2_b} - sc2;\nwire [31:0] pr3 = ua3 * {20'b0, mul3_b} - sc3;\nwire [31:0] pr4 = ua4 * {20'b0, mul4_b} - sc4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk )\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul1_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul2_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul4_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= pr0;\n    mul1_q <= pr1;\n    mul2_q <= pr2;\n    mul3_q <= pr3;\n    mul4_q <= pr4;\nend")

[Step 40/60]
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// jpeg_idct_2d - full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder
// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg, Apache 2.0): row pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) ->
// double-buffered transpose -> column pass (OUT_SHIFT=15), composed
// exactly as in the original jpeg_idct.v (downstream ready tied high,
// row pass accepts no backpressure).
//
// Pipeline structure, case schedules, FSMs and arithmetic are kept
// bit-exact with the original RTL.  Optimizations (all provably value
// preserving for any 16-bit signed input):
//   * multiplier operand registers narrowed (16/17 x 13 for the x pass,
//     21/22 x 13 for the y pass) instead of 32 x 32; products are still
//     evaluated in the 32-bit signed assignment context so the low-32
//     wrap-around behaviour is identical,
//   * cosine constants kept in 13-bit registers,
//   * transpose RAM storage narrowed to 21 bits
//     (|x-pass output| <= 709424128 >>> 11 = 346350 < 2^19),
//   * y-pass output buffer narrowed to 17 bits
//     (result = 32-bit sum >>> 15 lies in [-65536, 65535]).
//-----------------------------------------------------------------

module jpeg_idct_2d
(
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);

localparam XW = 21;   // narrowed x-pass result width (|x| <= 346350 < 2^20)
localparam YW = 17;   // narrowed y-pass result width (|y| < 2^16)

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Row pass (jpeg_idct_x equivalent, OUT_SHIFT = 11)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
wire          x_valid_w;
wire [XW-1:0] x_data_w;
wire [  5:0]  x_idx_w;

jpeg_idct_1d #(
     .OUT_SHIFT(11)
    ,.IN_W(16)
    ,.OUT_W(XW)
    ,.IDX_SWAP(1'b0)
) u_idct_x (
     .clk(clk)
    ,.rst(rst)
    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)
    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)
    ,.inport_valid_i(inport_valid_i)
    ,.inport_data0_i(inport_data0_i)
    ,.inport_data1_i(inport_data1_i)
    ,.inport_data2_i(inport_data2_i)
    ,.inport_data3_i(inport_data3_i)
    ,.inport_idx_i(inport_idx_i)
    ,.outport_valid_o(x_valid_w)
    ,.outport_data_o(x_data_w)
    ,.outport_idx_o(x_idx_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Double-buffered corner turn
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
wire          t_valid_w;
wire [XW-1:0] t_data0_w;
wire [XW-1:0] t_data1_w;
wire [XW-1:0] t_data2_w;
wire [XW-1:0] t_data3_w;
wire [  2:0]  t_idx_w;

jpeg_idct_transpose #(

[ASSISTANT]
The design is finalized and verified. **Final result: ADP = 5,564,328 — a 41.4% improvement over the original starting point (9,496,575)**, with 100% correctness (20,672/20,672 golden-vector checks).

## Summary of the work

**Final design (`design.sv`)** — bit-exact reimplementation of the core_jpeg 2-D IDCT (row pass → double-buffered transpose → column pass), preserving the original pipeline structure, case schedules, FSMs, and all 32-bit wrap-around arithmetic semantics (including the signed, truncating `(x*181)/256` odd-part scaling — its signed semantics were confirmed empirically against the golden vectors).

**Key optimizations, each verified by evaluation:**

1. **Registered one-hot transpose read** (the big win, −39% ADP): the original critical path was `rd_addr_q → 32:1 read mux`, where the address flops drive ~1000+ gate loads (~1.5 ns of fan-out delay alone). The read now registers a *one-hot decode of the next read address* (mirroring the address/FSM next-state logic exactly, preserving the one-cycle latency and read-first behavior) and uses a low fan-out AND-OR read. Delay 2222 → 1412 ps.

2. **Proven-safe width narrowing**: transpose storage 32→21 bits (x-pass outputs bounded by |≤ 709424128 >>> 11| = 346,350 — a bound that holds for *any* 16-bit input), x-pass output buffer/stream 21 bits, y-pass output buffer 17 bits (output = 32-bit sum >>> 15 ∈ [−65536, 65535]), cosine constants in 12-bit registers.

3. **Multipliers**: operands narrowed to (IN_W+1)×12 sign/zero-extended expression form (the factor is always a non-negative 12-bit constant), formed in the 32-bit signed context so low-32 wrap-around is identical to the original 32×32 product.

4. **Scaling datapath**: the `/256` signed division replaced by an explicit arithmetic shift + one-bit truncation correction, letting abc map the joint constant-multiply better.

Experiments that were tried and measurably reverted: hand-written CSA scale trees (worse mapping than abc's), unsigned-multiply + post-correction formulation, 20-bit storage (area −117 but consistently slower mapping), and target_delay sweeps (mapping is deterministic and insensitive below ~1400 ps).

**Final metrics**: area 4090.0, delay 1360.5 ps, area·delay product **5,564,328** (vs. 4,273.7 / 2222.1 ps / 9,496,575 for the untouched original).

[ASSISTANT]
Step 45. The evaluation exposed a real bug: 3 off-by-one failures. Analysis: my reformulation used `trunc(z−m) = trunc(z) − m`, which is **false when z and z−m straddle zero** (trunc isn't translation-invariant across zero) — exactly the wrap-crossing cases. The correct correction term uses the *wrapped* product's sign, which equals bit 23 of `T = floor(p/256)`. Fixing:
  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', new_string="// signed (x * 181) / 256, truncating toward zero, product wrapped at\n// 32 bits (bit-exact with the original fixed-point scaling).\n// Write x = 256*hi + lo (hi = x[31:8], lo = x[7:0]), p = 181*x exact:\n//   T := floor(p/256) = 181*hi + floor(181*lo/256)\n//   p mod 256 = (181*lo) mod 256 =: r\n// For n = p mod 2^32 (signed), n[7:0] = r and n[31] = T[23]\n// (n = (T<<8) + r - k*2^32, r occupies only bits [7:0]).\n// Hence trunc(n/256) = floor(n/256) + (n<0 & n[7:0]!=0)\n//                    = T - k*2^24 + (T[23] & (r!=0)),\n// whose unique representative in [-2^23, 2^23) is the low 24 bits of\n// T + (T[23] & (r!=0)) sign-extended.  Only low-24-bit const-multiply\n// arithmetic is required.\nfunction signed [31:0] scale_half;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg [7:0]  lo;\n    reg [15:0] b;\n    reg [7:0]  q;\n    reg [7:0]  r;\n    reg [23:0] a;\n    reg [23:0] s0;\n    reg [23:0] s;\n    reg        adj;\n    begin\n        lo = x[7:0];\n        // b = 181 * lo (exact, <= 16 bit): 128+32+16+4+1\n        b  = ({8'b0, lo} << 7) + ({8'b0, lo} << 5) +\n             ({8'b0, lo} << 4) + ({8'b0, lo} << 2) + {8'b0, lo};\n        q  = b[15:8];\n        r  = b[7:0];\n        // a = low 24 bits of 181 * hi (24-bit two's complement pattern)\n        a  = (x[31:8] << 7) + (x[31:8] << 5) +\n             (x[31:8] << 4) + (x[31:8] << 2) + x[31:8];\n        s0 = a + {16'b0, q};\n        adj = s0[23] & (|r);\n        s  = s0 + {23'b0, adj};\n        scale_half = {{8{s[23]}}, s};\n    end\nendfunction", old_string="// signed (x * 181) / 256, truncating toward zero, product wrapped at\n// 32 bits (bit-exact with the original fixed-point scaling).\n// Write x = 256*hi + lo (hi = x[31:8] signed, lo = x[7:0] unsigned).\n// Then p = 181*x = 256*(181*hi) + 181*lo, so\n//   floor(p/256) = 181*hi + floor(181*lo/256)      (181*lo >= 0)\n//   trunc(p/256) = floor(p/256) + adj,  adj = (x<0) & ((181*lo)%256 != 0)\n// and the wrapped result R = trunc((p mod 2^32)/256) is the unique\n// representative of trunc(p/256) in [-2^23, 2^23), i.e. the low 24 bits\n// sign-extended. [ASSISTANT]
Step 47: now functionally exact (0/20672), area −52, but delay regressed badly (1644 vs 1360) — the hi/lo split introduces a second serial CPA before the inherent increment. Worse ADP. Revert to the known-best trunc_div256 formulation:
  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', new_string="// signed division of a 32-bit value by 256, truncating toward zero\nfunction signed [31:0] trunc_div256;\n    input signed [31:0] w;\n    reg [23:0] s;\n    begin\n        s = w[31:8] + {23'b0, (w[31] & (|w[7:0]))};\n        trunc_div256 = {{8{s[23]}}, s};\n    end\nendfunction", old_string="// signed (x * 181) / 256, truncating toward zero, product wrapped at\n// 32 bits (bit-exact with the original fixed-point scaling).\n// Write x = 256*hi + lo (hi = x[31:8], lo = x[7:0]), p = 181*x exact:\n//   T := floor(p/256) = 181*hi + floor(181*lo/256)\n//   p mod 256 = (181*lo) mod 256 =: r\n// For n = p mod 2^32 (signed), n[7:0] = r and n[31] = T[23]\n// (n = (T<<8) + r - k*2^32, r occupies only bits [7:0]).\n// Hence trunc(n/256) = floor(n/256) + (n<0 & n[7:0]!=0)\n//                    = T - k*2^24 + (T[23] & (r!=0)),\n// whose unique representative in [-2^23, 2^23) is the low 24 bits of\n// T + (T[23] & (r!=0)) sign-extended.  Only low-24-bit const-multiply\n// arithmetic is required.\nfunction signed [31:0] scale_half;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg [7:0]  lo;\n    reg [15:0] b;\n    reg [7:0]  q;\n    reg [7:0]  r;\n    reg [23:0] a;\n    reg [23:0] s0;\n    reg [23:0] s;\n    reg        adj;\n    begin\n        lo = x[7:0];\n        // b = 181 * lo (exact, <= 16 bit): 128+32+16+4+1\n        b  = ({8'b0, lo} << 7) + ({8'b0, lo} << 5) +\n             ({8'b0, lo} << 4) + ({8'b0, lo} << 2) + {8'b0, lo};\n        q  = b[15:8];\n        r  = b[7:0];\n        // a = low 24 bits of 181 * hi (24-bit two's complement pattern)\n        a  = (x[31:8] << 7) + (x[31:8] << 5) +\n             (x[31:8] << 4) + (x[31:8] << 2) + x[31:8];\n        s0 = a + {16'b0, q};\n        adj = s0[23] & (|r);\n        s  = s0 + {23'b0, adj};\n        scale_half = {{8{s[23]}}, s};\n    end\nendfunction")

[ASSISTANT]
The design is finalized and fully verified. Final state:

## Result

**`design.sv` — 100% correct (20,672/20,672 golden checks), area·delay product = 5,564,328**

| | Original starting point | Final design | Δ |
|---|---|---|---|
| **ADP** | 9,496,575 | **5,564,328** | **−41.4%** |
| Area | 4273.7 | 4090.0 | −4.3% |
| Delay | 2222.1 ps | 1360.5 ps | −38.8% |

## What the final optimization rounds showed

- **Critical-path rebuild (the dominant win):** replacing the original `rd_addr_q → 32:1 RAM read mux` with a *registered one-hot next-address decode + low fan-out AND-OR read* cut delay from 2222 → 1412 ps (the address flops previously drove ~1000 gate loads, costing ~1.5 ns in fan-out alone).
- **Scale datapath:** the `(x*181)/256` fixed-point scaling was re-expressed as *arithmetic shift + one-bit truncation correction* (1360 ps vs 1412 for the raw operator), keeping the signed, truncate-toward-zero semantics bit-exact.
- **A deeper reformulation was attempted and rejected on merit:** splitting the multiply as `x = 256·hi + lo` proved *functionally* tricky — my first version exposed a genuine edge case (3 off-by-one failures) where `trunc(z−m) = trunc(z) − m` fails when the wrapped product crosses zero; I derived the exact correction (`adj = T[23] & (r≠0)`), got it to 0 errors, but its double-serial-CPA structure mapped to 1644 ps → reverted.
- **Width narrowing kept** where provably safe for *all* 16-bit inputs: transpose storage 32→21 bits, y-output buffer →17 bits, cosine constants in 12-bit registers, narrowed multiplier operands.
- **Lottery experiments documented:** XW=20 storage (−102 area but deterministically +125 ps delay, twice), and `target_delay` sweeps (200–1400) — the plateau at ≤1100 ps target is optimal; higher targets map slower.

The remaining critical path is the inherent constant-multiply + carry chain of the AAN odd-part scaling (~1360 ps), which multiple structurally-different formulations failed to beat — the design sits at the practical floor for this architecture and flow.

[Step 58/60]

// signed division of a 32-bit value by 256, truncating toward zero
function signed [31:0] trunc_div256;
    input signed [31:0] w;
    reg [23:0] s;
    begin
        s = w[31:8] + {23'b0, (w[31] & (|w[7:0]))};
        trunc_div256 = {{8{s[23]}}, s};
    end
endfunction

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4;        // s1

... (showing lines 395-454 of 824; use offset/limit for more)

## Summary

**Final: `design.sv` — PASS (20,672/20,672 checks), ADP 5,564,328 vs. baseline 9,496,575 (−41.4%)** (area 4090.0, delay 1360.5 ps).

### 1. Approaches tried / what worked
- **Restructured the transpose read path** (biggest win): replaced `rd_addr_q → 32:1 RAM mux` with a *registered one-hot decode of the next read address* (mirroring the rd_addr/block_rd next-state logic exactly) feeding a low fan-out AND-OR read. Same one-cycle latency, read-first semantics preserved.
- **Provably-safe width narrowing** from range proofs over all 16-bit inputs: transpose storage 32→21 bits (|x-pass out| ≤ 709424128>>>11 = 346,350), y output buffer →17 bits (sum>>>15 ∈ [−65536, 65535]), cosine constants →12-bit registers, narrowed multiplier operands (sign-extend expression form, 32-bit product context kept for wrap semantics).
- **Reformulated the critical `(x*181)/256` scaling** as `arithmetic shift right 8 + one-bit trunc-toward-zero correction` (`trunc_div256()` in design.sv) — bit-exact, −50 ps.
- Extensive measurement: keep-probes to decompose area, target_delay sweeps, and A/B trials of every structural variant.

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
1. One-hot registered read: delay 2222 → 1412 ps alone (−39% ADP in one step).
2. Scale-path re-expression: 1412 → 1360 ps.
3. Width narrowing: −150 area units at zero delay cost.
Multiplier formulation experiments confirmed the expression-level sign-extended form was already area-competitive.

### 3. What didn't work / regressed
- **hi/lo split of the ×181 scaling**: first attempt had a genuine bug (3 off-by-one failures) — `trunc(z−m) = trunc(z) − m` fails when the wrapped product crosses zero; fixed exactly via `adj = T[23] & (r≠0)`, but its double-serial-CPA structure mapped to 1644 ps → reverted.
- Hand-written CSA trees for the scale (1485, 1600 ps) — abc's techmap of the plain `x * 181` expression mapped better (1360).
- XW=20 storage: −102 area but deterministically +125 ps delay (twice) → net loss.
- target_delay > 1200: progressively worse (1250→5.62M, 1350→5.79M, 1400→5.95M); targets ≤1200 are one identical plateau.
- Unsigned-multiply + post-correction multiplier: no area gain (extension folding never materialized as hoped).

### 4. Lessons
- **Measure, don't assume**: keep-probes and A/B evals repeatedly falsified my mental model of area composition; mapping delay is deterministic per (code, target) but swings ±10% on small structural changes — treat every variant as a sample and always keep the running best.
- The testbench is a strong bit-exact oracle — semantic-risky reformulations are cheap to validate, but proof-carrying bounds (my range arguments) let you narrow widths safely.
- **Next time**: I'd instrument area composition (probes) *first*, and I'd attempt the scale-path fix (shift+correction) earlier since it sits on the critical path; I'd also explore folding the truncation correction into the constant-multiply's carry chain, which I identified but couldn't reach within the step budget.
